```
        var report = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
        report.AppendLine($"[report] player {position} chunk {coord} surface {_scene.SurfaceHeight(position.X, position.Z)} elapsed {_scene.ElapsedTime:F2}s grounded {_scene.Player.IsGrounded}");

        var solidAbove = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
        for (int x = (int)MathF.Floor(position.X - 0.3f); x <= (int)MathF.Floor(position.X + 0.3f); x++)
        for (int z = (int)MathF.Floor(position.Z - 0.3f); z <= (int)MathF.Floor(position.Z + 0.3f); z++)
        for (int y = 12; y < _scene.World.Height; y++)
            if (BlockRegistry.IsSolid(_scene.World.GetBlock(x, y, z))) solidAbove.Append($" ({x},{y},{z})");
        report.AppendLine($"[report] solid blocks above y=12 under the player:{solidAbove}");
        report.AppendLine($"[report] loaded {_scene.World.LoadedChunkCount} pending {_scene.World.PendingLoads} meshed {_scene.Meshes.MeshedChunks} queue {_scene.Meshes.PendingChunks} tris {_scene.Meshes.TotalTriangles} gpu {_scene.Meshes.GpuBytes / (1024 * 1024)} MB draws {_scene.Meshes.DrawCalls}");
        for (int dz = -1; dz <= 1; dz++)
        for (int dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++)
            report.AppendLine("[report] " + _scene.Meshes.Describe(new ChunkCoord(coord.X + dx, coord.Z + dz)));

        return report.ToString();
    }

    public override bool SaveGame() => _scene.Save();

    public override bool LoadGame() => _scene.Load();

    public override void Unload() => _scene.Dispose();
}
```

### Games/InstantCamera/Scripts/GalleryGenerator.cs
```
using System.Numerics;
using VoxelEngine.MathTools;
using VoxelEngine.World;

namespace Games.InstantCamera;

/// <summary>The gallery's materials; registered while loading, before the world is generated</summary>
internal readonly record struct GalleryMaterials(byte Plinth, byte Marble, byte Brick, byte Gold, byte Wood, byte Leaves);

/// <summary>
/// A flat meadow with a ring of plinths around the spawn, each carrying a shape worth
/// photographing from more than one side, and trees scattered out to the horizon. Flat on
/// purpose: a piece put down at an angle stands out clearly against level ground.
/// </summary>
internal sealed class GalleryGenerator : ITerrainGenerator
{
    public const int GroundHeight = 48;

    private const int TopsoilDepth = 3;
    private const float RingRadius = 22f;
    private const float PlinthRadius = 3.5f;
    private const int PlinthHeight = 2;

    private const int TreeCell = 12;
    private const float TreeChance = 0.22f;
    private const float ClearRadius = 34f;
    private const int Seed = 7031;

    /// <summary>A shape in a box of world blocks; Sample gets a block centre and returns its material or air</summary>
    private readonly record struct Shape(int MinX, int MinY, int MinZ, int MaxX, int MaxY, int MaxZ, Func<Vector3, byte> Sample);

    private readonly GalleryMaterials _materials;
    private readonly Shape[] _exhibits;
    private readonly Vector3[] _plinthTops;

    /// <summary>Centre of each plinth's top face, where its object stands</summary>
    public IReadOnlyList<Vector3> PlinthTops => _plinthTops;

    public GalleryGenerator(GalleryMaterials materials)
    {
        _materials = materials;

        Func<Vector3, byte>[] objects = { Arch, SpiralStair, Sphere, Ring, Tower, BigTree };
        _exhibits = new Shape[objects.Length];
        _plinthTops = new Vector3[objects.Length];

        for (int i = 0; i < objects.Length; i++)
        {
            float angle = (i + 0.5f) * MathF.Tau / objects.Length;
            int x = (int)MathF.Round(MathF.Cos(angle) * RingRadius);
            int z = (int)MathF.Round(MathF.Sin(angle) * RingRadius);

            _plinthTops[i] = new Vector3(x + 0.5f, GroundHeight + PlinthHeight, z + 0.5f);
            _exhibits[i] = OnPlinth(_plinthTops[i], angle, objects[i]);
        }
    }

    public void Generate(ChunkCoord coord, byte[] blocks, int worldHeight)
    {
        for (int y = 0; y < GroundHeight; y++)
        {
            byte id = y >= GroundHeight - TopsoilDepth ? BlockRegistry.Terrain : BlockRegistry.Stone;
            for (int z = 0; z < Chunk.Size; z++)
            for (int x = 0; x < Chunk.Size; x++)
                blocks[Chunk.Index(x, y, z)] = id;
        }

        foreach (Shape exhibit in _exhibits)
            Stamp(exhibit, coord, blocks, worldHeight);

        // Trees reach up to three blocks past their cell, so the cells around the chunk count too
        int originX = coord.X * Chunk.Size;
        int originZ = coord.Z * Chunk.Size;
        for (int cellZ = FloorDiv(originZ - 3, TreeCell); cellZ <= FloorDiv(originZ + Chunk.Size + 3, TreeCell); cellZ++)
        for (int cellX = FloorDiv(originX - 3, TreeCell); cellX <= FloorDiv(originX + Chunk.Size + 3, TreeCell); cellX++)
            if (TreeIn(cellX, cellZ) is { } tree)
                Stamp(tree, coord, blocks, worldHeight);
    }

    private static void Stamp(in Shape shape, ChunkCoord coord, byte[] blocks, int worldHeight)
    {
        int originX = coord.X * Chunk.Size;
        int originZ = coord.Z * Chunk.Size;

        int minX = Math.Max(shape.MinX, originX);
        int maxX = Math.Min(shape.MaxX, originX + Chunk.Size - 1);
        int minZ = Math.Max(shape.MinZ, originZ);
        int maxZ = Math.Min(shape.MaxZ, originZ + Chunk.Size - 1);
        int minY = Math.Max(shape.MinY, 0);
        int maxY = Math.Min(shape.MaxY, worldHeight - 1);

        for (int y = minY; y <= maxY; y++)
        for (int z = minZ; z <= maxZ; z++)
        for (int x = minX; x <= maxX; x++)
        {
            byte id = shape.Sample(new Vector3(x + 0.5f, y + 0.5f, z + 0.5f));
            if (id != BlockRegistry.Air) blocks[Chunk.Index(x - originX, y, z - originZ)] = id;
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// A plinth under <paramref name="top"/> with an object on it. The object sees coordinates
    /// from the centre of the plinth's top, with x running around the ring and z out from the
    /// centre, so its broad side faces the plaza.
    /// </summary>
    private Shape OnPlinth(Vector3 top, float angle, Func<Vector3, byte> exhibit)
    {
        int x = (int)MathF.Floor(top.X);
        int z = (int)MathF.Floor(top.Z);
        var around = new Vector2(-MathF.Sin(angle), MathF.Cos(angle));
        var outward = new Vector2(MathF.Cos(angle), MathF.Sin(angle));

        return new Shape(x - 5, GroundHeight, z - 5, x + 5, GroundHeight + PlinthHeight + 17, z + 5, point =>
        {
            Vector3 local = point - top;
            if (local.Y < 0f)
                return local.X * local.X + local.Z * local.Z <= PlinthRadius * PlinthRadius ? _materials.Plinth : BlockRegistry.Air;

            var flat = new Vector2(local.X, local.Z);
            return exhibit(new Vector3(Vector2.Dot(flat, around), local.Y, Vector2.Dot(flat, outward)));
        });
    }

    private byte Arch(Vector3 p)
    {
        if (MathF.Abs(p.Z) > 1f) return BlockRegistry.Air;

        bool leg = p.Y < 4f && MathF.Abs(p.X) is >= 2f and <= 3.2f;
        float bend = MathF.Sqrt(p.X * p.X + (p.Y - 4f) * (p.Y - 4f));
        bool span = p.Y >= 4f && bend is >= 2f and <= 3.2f;

        return leg || span ? _materials.Marble : BlockRegistry.Air;
    }

    private byte SpiralStair(Vector3 p)
    {
        if (p.Y >= 12f) return BlockRegistry.Air;

        float radius = MathF.Sqrt(p.X * p.X + p.Z * p.Z);
        if (radius < 0.8f) return _materials.Brick;
        if (radius > 3.2f) return BlockRegistry.Air;

        // One step per block of height, each a slice of the circle a twelfth further round
        float stepAngle = MathF.Floor(p.Y) * MathF.Tau / 12f;
        float difference = MathF.Abs(MathF.IEEERemainder(MathF.Atan2(p.Z, p.X) - stepAngle, MathF.Tau));
        return difference < MathF.PI / 7f ? _materials.Brick : BlockRegistry.Air;
    }

    private byte Sphere(Vector3 p)
    {
        Vector3 center = new(0f, 3.2f, 0f);
        return Vector3.DistanceSquared(p, center) <= 9f ? _materials.Gold : BlockRegistry.Air;
    }

    private byte Ring(Vector3 p)
    {
        float around = MathF.Sqrt(p.X * p.X + (p.Y - 4f) * (p.Y - 4f)) - 3f;
        return around * around + p.Z * p.Z <= 0.9f * 0.9f ? _materials.Marble : BlockRegistry.Air;
    }

    private byte Tower(Vector3 p)
    {
        if (MathF.Abs(p.X) > 1.5f || MathF.Abs(p.Z) > 1.5f) return BlockRegistry.Air;
        if (p.Y < 14f) return _materials.Brick;
        if (p.Y >= 15f) return BlockRegistry.Air;

        // Battlements: every other block around the edge of the top
        int cornerX = (int)MathF.Floor(p.X);
        int cornerZ = (int)MathF.Floor(p.Z);
        bool edge = MathF.Abs(p.X) > 0.5f || MathF.Abs(p.Z) > 0.5f;
        return edge && ((cornerX + cornerZ) & 1) == 0 ? _materials.Brick : BlockRegistry.Air;
    }

    private byte BigTree(Vector3 p) => Tree(p, trunkHeight: 7, crownRadius: 3.6f);

    private byte Tree(Vector3 p, int trunkHeight, float crownRadius)
    {
        if (MathF.Abs(p.X) < 0.6f && MathF.Abs(p.Z) < 0.6f && p.Y < trunkHeight) return _materials.Wood;

        Vector3 crown = new(0f, trunkHeight + 0.5f, 0f);
        return Vector3.DistanceSquared(p, crown) <= crownRadius * crownRadius ? _materials.Leaves : BlockRegistry.Air;
    }

    /// <summary>The tree of one cell of the scattering grid, or none</summary>
    private Shape? TreeIn(int cellX, int cellZ)
    {
        if (Noise.Value2D(cellX, cellZ, Seed) > TreeChance) return null;

        int x = cellX * TreeCell + 3 + (int)(Noise.Value2D(cellX, cellZ, Seed + 1) * (TreeCell - 6));
        int z = cellZ * TreeCell + 3 + (int)(Noise.Value2D(cellX, cellZ, Seed + 2) * (TreeCell - 6));
        if (x * x + z * z < ClearRadius * ClearRadius) return null;

        int trunk = 4 + (int)(Noise.Value2D(cellX, cellZ, Seed + 3) * 3f);
        float crown = 2.2f + Noise.Value2D(cellX, cellZ, Seed + 4) * 0.8f;
        var foot = new Vector3(x + 0.5f, GroundHeight, z + 0.5f);

        return new Shape(x - 3, GroundHeight, z - 3, x + 3, GroundHeight + trunk + 4, z + 3,
            point => Tree(point - foot, trunk, crown));
    }

    private static int FloorDiv(int value, int divisor)
        => value >= 0 ? value / divisor : -((-value + divisor - 1) / divisor);
}
```

### Games/InstantCamera/Scripts/Hands.cs
```
using Raylib_cs;
using System.Numerics;
using VoxelEngine.MathTools;

namespace Games.InstantCamera;

/// <summary>
/// What the player holds in first person: the instant camera on the right, the print on the left,
/// and the moves between them. Raising the camera ends in the viewfinder; raising the print ends
/// with it exactly over the part of the view it would replace. Whichever is raised, the other
/// gets out of its way. This class only animates and draws; when to shoot or put a print down is
/// the game's call.
/// </summary>
internal sealed class Hands : IDisposable
{
    private const float CameraSeconds = 0.3f;
    private const float PrintSeconds = 0.28f;
    private const float ArriveSeconds = 0.45f;
    private const float FlashSeconds = 0.25f;
    private const float FadeSeconds = 0.4f;

    /// <summary>How far in front of the eye the raised print is held; its picture fits the frame exactly there</summary>
    public const float RaisedDistance = 0.15f;

    /// <summary>Distance of the viewfinder mask, the flash and anything else laid over the view</summary>
    private const float OverlayDistance = 0.11f;

    private static readonly Placement CameraLowered =
        new(new Vector3(0.22f, -0.15f, -0.52f), Quaternion.CreateFromYawPitchRoll(0.35f, 0.14f, 0.04f));

    private static readonly Placement CameraAtEye = new(new Vector3(0f, -0.01f, -0.14f), Quaternion.Identity);

    private static readonly Placement PrintLowered =
        new(new Vector3(-0.2f, -0.12f, -0.46f), Quaternion.CreateFromYawPitchRoll(0.3f, -0.45f, 0.06f));

    private float _cameraUp;
    private float _printUp;
    private float _arrive = 1f;
    private float _flash;
    private float _walk;
    private float _walkPhase;

    private Photograph? _fading;
    private float _fadingRoll;
    private float _fade;

    /// <summary>The camera is at the eye: the viewfinder is all the player sees</summary>
    public bool CameraUp => _cameraUp >= 1f;

    /// <summary>The print has arrived in the middle of the view</summary>
    public bool PrintUp => _printUp >= 1f;

    /// <summary>One frame of animation towards where camera and print are wanted</summary>
    public void Update(float dt, bool cameraToEye, bool printUp, float walkSpeed, bool grounded)
    {
        _cameraUp = Approach(_cameraUp, cameraToEye ? 1f : 0f, dt / CameraSeconds);
        _printUp = Approach(_printUp, printUp ? 1f : 0f, dt / PrintSeconds);
        _arrive = MathF.Min(1f, _arrive + dt / ArriveSeconds);
        _flash = MathF.Max(0f, _flash - dt / FlashSeconds);

        if (_fading != null)
        {
            _fade -= dt / FadeSeconds;
            if (_fade <= 0f)
            {
                _fading.Dispose();
                _fading = null;
            }
        }

        float walking = grounded ? Math.Clamp(walkSpeed / 7f, 0f, 1.3f) : 0f;
        _walk += (walking - _walk) * MathF.Min(1f, dt * 8f);
        _walkPhase += dt * walkSpeed * 1.7f;
    }

    /// <summary>The shutter went: flash, and a fresh print comes up into the left hand</summary>
    public void Shot()
    {
        _flash = 1f;
        _arrive = 0f;
        _printUp = 0f;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// The raised print was put down: it stays where it was held and dissolves into the world
    /// that now matches it. From here on it is this class's to release when it is gone.
    /// </summary>
    public void Placed(Photograph print, float roll)
    {
        _fading?.Dispose();
        _fading = print;
        _fadingRoll = roll;
        _fade = 1f;
        _printUp = 0f;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Draws camera and print for the eye at <paramref name="eye"/>. <paramref name="frameTanHalf"/> is
    /// the viewfinder's opening, <paramref name="fovY"/> and <paramref name="aspect"/> those of
    /// the view, so the viewfinder mask covers all of it.
    /// </summary>
    public void Draw(ViewModelPainter painter, CameraPose eye, Photograph? print, float roll,
        float frameTanHalf, float fovY, float aspect)
    {
        painter.Begin(eye);

        var bob = new Vector3(MathF.Sin(_walkPhase) * 0.006f, -MathF.Abs(MathF.Cos(_walkPhase)) * 0.012f, 0f) * _walk;

        // Past this the camera is at the face and the viewfinder mask has closed over it
        if (_cameraUp < 0.9f)
        {
            float up = Ease(_cameraUp);

            // The camera goes down out of the way while the print is up, which needs the view to itself
            Vector3 lowered = bob + new Vector3(0.05f, -0.32f, 0f) * Ease(_printUp);
            Placement camera = Placement.Lerp(CameraLowered.Moved(lowered), CameraAtEye, up)
                .Moved(new Vector3(0f, MathF.Sin(up * MathF.PI) * 0.05f, 0f)); // up through the middle, not straight across

            CameraModel.Draw(painter, camera);
        }

        if (print != null)
        {
            float half = print.TanHalf * RaisedDistance;
            float up = Ease(_printUp);

            // A new print comes up from below, and the held one goes down while the camera is
            // at the eye, or it would show in the viewfinder. A raised print must not bob, or it
            // would not fit the frame.
            float away = (1f - Ease(_arrive)) * 0.3f + Ease(_cameraUp) * 0.4f;
            Placement lowered = PrintLowered.Moved(bob * (1f - up) - Vector3.UnitY * away);
            Placement placement = Placement.Lerp(lowered, Raised(roll), up);

            PrintModel.Draw(painter, placement, print, half, 1f);
        }

        if (_fading != null)
            PrintModel.Draw(painter, Raised(_fadingRoll), _fading, _fading.TanHalf * RaisedDistance, Ease(_fade));

        painter.BeginOverlay();

        float mask = SmoothStep(0.55f, 0.95f, _cameraUp);
        if (mask > 0f) DrawViewfinder(painter, mask, frameTanHalf, fovY, aspect);

        if (_flash > 0f)
        {
            (float halfWidth, float halfHeight) = ViewHalfSize(fovY, aspect);
            painter.Quad(Placement.Identity, -halfWidth, halfWidth, -halfHeight, halfHeight, -OverlayDistance,
                new Color(255, 255, 255, (int)(Ease(_flash) * 200f)));
        }

        painter.End();
    }

    /// <summary>The print in the middle of the view, turned by the roll</summary>
    private static Placement Raised(float roll)
        => new(new Vector3(0f, 0f, -RaisedDistance), Quaternion.CreateFromAxisAngle(Vector3.UnitZ, roll));

    /// <summary>Dark all round the square frame, and white marks at its corners</summary>
    private static void DrawViewfinder(ViewModelPainter painter, float strength, float frameTanHalf, float fovY, float aspect)
    {
        (float halfWidth, float halfHeight) = ViewHalfSize(fovY, aspect);
        float frame = frameTanHalf * OverlayDistance;
        const float z = -OverlayDistance;

        var dark = new Color(8, 9, 12, (int)(strength * 225f));
        painter.Quad(Placement.Identity, -halfWidth, halfWidth, frame, halfHeight, z, dark);
        painter.Quad(Placement.Identity, -halfWidth, halfWidth, -halfHeight, -frame, z, dark);
        painter.Quad(Placement.Identity, -halfWidth, -frame, -frame, frame, z, dark);
        painter.Quad(Placement.Identity, frame, halfWidth, -frame, frame, z, dark);

        var mark = new Color(255, 255, 255, (int)(strength * 220f));
        float arm = frame * 0.16f;
        float thick = frame * 0.012f;
        foreach ((float sx, float sy) in new[] { (-1f, -1f), (1f, -1f), (1f, 1f), (-1f, 1f) })
        {
            float x = sx * frame;
            float y = sy * frame;
            painter.Quad(Placement.Identity, MathF.Min(x, x - sx * arm), MathF.Max(x, x - sx * arm), MathF.Min(y, y - sy * thick), MathF.Max(y, y - sy * thick), z, mark);
            painter.Quad(Placement.Identity, MathF.Min(x, x - sx * thick), MathF.Max(x, x - sx * thick), MathF.Min(y, y - sy * arm), MathF.Max(y, y - sy * arm), z, mark);
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Half the view's width and height at the overlay distance, with a margin so nothing peeks past the edge</summary>
    private static (float HalfWidth, float HalfHeight) ViewHalfSize(float fovY, float aspect)
    {
        float halfHeight = MathF.Tan(fovY * 0.5f * MathF.PI / 180f) * OverlayDistance * 1.1f;
        return (halfHeight * aspect, halfHeight);
    }

    private static float Approach(float value, float target, float step)
        => value < target ? MathF.Min(target, value + step) : MathF.Max(target, value - step);

    private static float Ease(float t) => t * t * (3f - 2f * t);

    private static float SmoothStep(float from, float to, float value) => Ease(Math.Clamp((value - from) / (to - from), 0f, 1f));

    public void Dispose() => _fading?.Dispose();
}
```

### Games/InstantCamera/Scripts/HeldItems.cs
```
using Raylib_cs;

namespace Games.InstantCamera;

/// <summary>The instant camera, built from boxes like the world around it</summary>
internal static class CameraModel
{
    private static readonly Color Body = new(232, 228, 216, 255);
    private static readonly Color Housing = new(46, 46, 50, 255);
    private static readonly Color Black = new(22, 22, 24, 255);
    private static readonly Color Glass = new(44, 62, 84, 255);
    private static readonly Color Shutter = new(206, 46, 40, 255);

    private static readonly Color[] Rainbow =
    {
        new(214, 62, 52, 255), new(236, 140, 44, 255), new(240, 204, 60, 255), new(92, 170, 82, 255), new(58, 118, 196, 255),
    };

    /// <summary>The camera centred on its frame's origin, lens towards -z, the back with the eyepiece towards the player</summary>
    public static void Draw(ViewModelPainter painter, in Placement camera)
    {
        painter.Box(camera, new(-0.08f, -0.055f, -0.06f), new(0.08f, 0.045f, 0.06f), Body);
        painter.Box(camera, new(-0.08f, 0.045f, -0.06f), new(0.08f, 0.08f, 0.06f), Housing);
        painter.Box(camera, new(-0.058f, 0.052f, 0.06f), new(-0.022f, 0.074f, 0.07f), Black);  // eyepiece
        painter.Box(camera, new(0.034f, 0.08f, -0.048f), new(0.062f, 0.09f, -0.02f), Shutter);
        painter.Box(camera, new(-0.036f, -0.036f, -0.085f), new(0.036f, 0.036f, -0.06f), Black); // lens barrel
        painter.Box(camera, new(-0.024f, -0.024f, -0.088f), new(0.024f, 0.024f, -0.085f), Glass);

        // The rainbow stripe, the mark of an instant camera, down the back where the player sees it
        for (int i = 0; i < Rainbow.Length; i++)
        {
            float x = -0.07f + i * 0.006f;
            painter.Box(camera, new(x, -0.045f, 0.06f), new(x + 0.006f, 0.035f, 0.063f), Rainbow[i]);
        }
    }
}

/// <summary>A print: the picture in a paper rim, with the wide strip at the bottom that it is held by</summary>
internal static class PrintModel
{
    /// <summary>Width of the rim as a share of half the picture's side</summary>
    public const float RimShare = 0.06f;

    /// <summary>The bottom strip is this many rims tall</summary>
    public const float StripRims = 3.5f;

    private static readonly Color Paper = new(246, 244, 236, 255);

    /// <summary>The grey-brown of instant film before the picture comes through</summary>
    private static readonly Color Unexposed = new(96, 90, 80, 255);

    /// <summary>The print facing +z in its frame, the picture <paramref name="half"/> metres from centre to edge</summary>
    public static void Draw(ViewModelPainter painter, in Placement print, Photograph photograph, float half, float alpha)
    {
        float rim = half * RimShare;
        float strip = rim * StripRims;
        Color paper = Fade(Paper, alpha);

        // The rim in four strips around the picture rather than one sheet behind it, so a print
        // fading out does not show paper through the picture
        painter.Quad(print, -half - rim, half + rim, half, half + rim, 0f, paper);
        painter.Quad(print, -half - rim, half + rim, -half - strip, -half, 0f, paper);
        painter.Quad(print, -half - rim, -half, -half, half, 0f, paper);
        painter.Quad(print, half, half + rim, -half, half, 0f, paper);

        painter.Quad(print, -half, half, -half, half, 0f, Fade(Color.White, alpha), photograph.Picture.Id);
        painter.Quad(print, -half, half, -half, half, 0.001f, Fade(Unexposed, photograph.Cover * alpha));
    }

    private static Color Fade(Color color, float alpha) => new(color.R, color.G, color.B, (byte)(Math.Clamp(alpha, 0f, 1f) * color.A));
}
```

### Games/InstantCamera/Scripts/InstantCameraGame.cs
```
using Raylib_cs;
using System.Numerics;
using VoxelEngine.Audio;
using VoxelEngine.Core;
using VoxelEngine.Input;
using VoxelEngine.MathTools;
using VoxelEngine.Scenes;
using VoxelEngine.UI;
using VoxelEngine.World;

namespace Games.InstantCamera;

/// <summary>
/// An instant camera for the voxel world, after Viewfinder: photograph a piece of the world
/// through the square frame, hold the print up somewhere else, and put it down. What was in the
/// frame there makes way, and the photographed pieces stand where the picture shows them, at
/// whatever angle it was held. The pieces are free voxel bodies with their own grids, so they
/// keep every block and every sculpted detail at any angle.
/// </summary>
[GameDefinition("InstantCamera", "Instant Camera", "Photograph a piece of the world and put it down somewhere else")]
public sealed class InstantCameraGame : Game
{
    /// <summary>Side of the square viewfinder as a share of the screen height</summary>
    private const float FrameShare = 0.62f;

    /// <summary>Radians per second the raised print turns while Q or E is held</summary>
    private const float RollSpeed = 1.4f;

    private const float MinDepth = 4f;
    private const float MaxDepth = 96f;

    private VoxelTerrainScene _scene = null!;
    private ViewModelPainter _painter = null!;
    private readonly Hands _hands = new();

    private Photograph? _photo;
    private bool _aiming;
    private bool _shootWhenUp;
    private bool _printRaised;
    private float _roll;
    private float _depth = 40f;
    private Vector3 _lastPosition;

    public override Camera3D Camera => _scene.Camera;

    public override IReadOnlyList<string> ControlHints => new[]
    {
        "WASD + mouse: walk and look, Shift sprints, Space jumps, hold it for the jetpack",
        "Hold LMB: raise the camera to the eye and aim | let go: take the photograph",
        "RMB: raise the print, RMB again: lower it | LMB while raised: put it down | Q/E: turn it",
        "Wheel, or two fingers on a trackpad: how deep the photograph reaches, 4 to 96 m",
        "V: voxel mode | G: godmode | M: tuning | F3: debug",
        "K: record to the desktop | ESC settings: video size, in-game text",
    };

    public override void Load()
    {
        var materials = new GalleryMaterials(
            Plinth: BlockRegistry.Register("gallery.plinth", new Color(158, 154, 146, 255)),
            Marble: BlockRegistry.Register("gallery.marble", new Color(236, 232, 222, 255)),
            Brick: BlockRegistry.Register("gallery.brick", new Color(168, 74, 56, 255)),
            Gold: BlockRegistry.Register("gallery.gold", new Color(232, 184, 64, 255)),
            Wood: BlockRegistry.Register("gallery.wood", new Color(112, 80, 52, 255)),
            Leaves: BlockRegistry.Register("gallery.leaves", new Color(72, 138, 62, 255)));

        var gallery = new GalleryGenerator(materials);

        _scene = new VoxelTerrainScene(Context, new VoxelTerrainOptions
        {
            SaveSlot = "instant-camera",
            Generator = gallery,
            Spawn = new Vector3(0.5f, GalleryGenerator.GroundHeight + 1, 0.5f),
            Mode = TerrainMode.Blocks,
            AllowEditing = false,
            RunDayNight = false,
            SunAngleDegrees = 55f, // low enough that the shapes throw light and shade at each other
            Jetpack = true,
            Godmode = true,
        });

        // Pieces lie at any angle and meet the ground in small ledges: walk up those instead of
        // jumping at every one. A whole block still takes a jump.
        _scene.Player.StepHeight = 0.55f;

        // Start facing the exhibit the sun lights from behind the player, not into the light
        _scene.DayNight.Update(0f);
        var light = new Vector2(_scene.DayNight.SunDirection.X, _scene.DayNight.SunDirection.Z);
        Vector3 facing = gallery.PlinthTops.MaxBy(top => Vector2.Dot(new Vector2(top.X, top.Z), light));
        _scene.Player.PointAt(facing + Vector3.UnitY * 3f);
        _lastPosition = _scene.Player.Position;

        _painter = new ViewModelPainter();

        Context.Audio.Define("shutter", new SfxShape(0.09f, 2400f, 700f, 0.85f, 28f));
        Context.Audio.Define("place", new SfxShape(0.5f, 180f, 760f, 0.35f, 3.5f));
    }

    public override void Update(float dt)
    {
        if (Raylib.IsKeyPressed(KeyboardKey.V)) _scene.CycleMode();
        if (Raylib.IsKeyPressed(KeyboardKey.G)) ToggleGodmode();

        bool leftPressed = Raylib.IsMouseButtonPressed(MouseButton.Left);
        bool leftDown = Raylib.IsMouseButtonDown(MouseButton.Left);
        bool rightPressed = Raylib.IsMouseButtonPressed(MouseButton.Right);

        // A click raises the print and the next lowers it: holding one button while clicking the
        // other does not work on a trackpad
        if (_photo == null) _printRaised = false;
        else if (rightPressed && !_aiming) _printRaised = !_printRaised;

        bool place = false;

        if (_printRaised)
        {
            float turn = (Raylib.IsKeyDown(KeyboardKey.Q) ? 1f : 0f) - (Raylib.IsKeyDown(KeyboardKey.E) ? 1f : 0f);
            _roll += turn * RollSpeed * dt;

            place = leftPressed && _hands.PrintUp;
        }
        else
        {
            // Holding the button brings the camera to the eye; letting go takes the photograph,
            // at once if the camera is up, or the moment it gets there after a quick click
            if (leftPressed)
            {
                _aiming = true;
                _shootWhenUp = false;
            }

            if (_aiming && !leftDown) _shootWhenUp = true;

            float wheel = Raylib.GetMouseWheelMove();
            if (wheel != 0f)
            {
                _depth = Math.Clamp(_depth + wheel * 2f, MinDepth, MaxDepth);
                Context.ShowStatus($"Photo depth {_depth:0} m"); // the status line shows even without the in-game readouts
            }
        }

        _scene.ShowDebugGeometry = Context.DebugOverlay;
        _scene.Update(dt);

        Vector3 position = _scene.Player.Position;
        float walked = new Vector2(position.X - _lastPosition.X, position.Z - _lastPosition.Z).Length();
        _lastPosition = position;
        _hands.Update(dt, _aiming, _printRaised, dt > 0f ? walked / dt : 0f, _scene.Player.IsGrounded && !_scene.Godmode);

        // After the scene's step, so the photograph and the print go with this frame's camera
        if (place) PlacePhoto();
        if (_aiming && _shootWhenUp && _hands.CameraUp) TakePhoto();

        _photo?.Develop(dt);
    }

    public override void UpdateAlways() => _scene.PumpMeshUploads();

    public override void DrawBackground() => _scene.DrawBackground();

    public override void DrawWorld()
    {
        _scene.Draw();

        _hands.Draw(_painter, CameraPose.FromCamera(_scene.Camera), _photo, _roll, TanHalf(),
            _scene.Camera.FovY, Context.ScreenWidth / (float)Context.ScreenHeight);
    }

    private void TakePhoto()
    {
        _photo?.Dispose();
        _photo = Photograph.Take(_scene, _scene.Camera, TanHalf(), _depth);
        _aiming = false;
        _roll = 0f;

        _hands.Shot();
        Context.Audio.Play("shutter", 0.7f, 1f);
    }

    private void PlacePhoto()
    {
        _photo!.Place(CameraPose.FromCamera(_scene.Camera, _roll));
        _hands.Placed(_photo, _roll);
        _photo = null;
        _printRaised = false;
        _roll = 0f;

        FreePlayer();
        Context.Audio.Play("place", 0.6f, 1f);
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// A piece put down around the player would hold them in place: lift them out on top of it.
    /// Past a few metres of rock they stay where they are, and the collision lets them walk out.
    /// </summary>
    private void FreePlayer()
    {
        if (_scene.Godmode) return;

        PlayerController player = _scene.Player;
        Vector3 start = player.Position;

        for (int step = 1; step <= 60 && _scene.BodiesOverlap(player.Bounds); step++)
            player.Teleport(start + Vector3.UnitY * (step * 0.1f));

        if (_scene.BodiesOverlap(player.Bounds)) player.Teleport(start);
    }

    /// <summary>The square frame's opening, from the camera's vertical field of view</summary>
    private float TanHalf() => MathF.Tan(_scene.Camera.FovY * 0.5f * MathF.PI / 180f) * FrameShare;

    private void ToggleGodmode()
    {
        _scene.Godmode = !_scene.Godmode;
        Context.ShowStatus(_scene.Godmode ? "Godmode on: fly with WASD, Space up, Ctrl down" : "Godmode off");
    }

    public override void DrawHud()
    {
        Hud.Text("Hold LMB aim, let go to shoot | RMB raise or lower the print, LMB put it down, Q/E turn it | Wheel depth", 10, 40, 20, Color.Black);
        Hud.Text("V mode | G godmode | Space jetpack | K record | F3 debug | M tuning | ESC menu", 10, 65, 20, Color.Black);

        if (Context.DebugOverlay)
            Hud.Text($"Pieces {_scene.Bodies.Count} | Photo {(_photo != null ? $"{_photo.Depth:0} m" : "none")}", 10, 90, 20, Color.DarkBlue);

        // Looking through the viewfinder, the frame is the aim; otherwise the crosshair is
        if (_hands.CameraUp)
        {
            float frameBottom = Context.ScreenHeight * (0.5f + FrameShare * 0.5f);
            Hud.Centered($"depth {_depth:0} m", Context.ScreenWidth / 2, (int)frameBottom + 10, 16);
        }
        else if (!_printRaised)
        {
            Hud.Crosshair(Context.ScreenWidth, Context.ScreenHeight);
        }

        ModeTransition? wave = _scene.Transition;
        ModeIndicator.Draw(Context.ScreenWidth / 2, Context.ScreenHeight - 74, _scene.Mode, _scene.SinceModeSwitch,
            wave?.Progress01(_scene.FogEnd) ?? 1f, wave != null && wave.Remeshes, "V");
    }

    public override string DebugReport()
        => $"[report] pieces {_scene.Bodies.Count} photo {(_photo != null ? "held" : "none")} player {_scene.Player.Position}";

    public override void Unload()
    {
        _photo?.Dispose();
        _hands.Dispose();
        _painter.Dispose();
        _scene.Dispose();
    }
}
```

### Games/InstantCamera/Scripts/Photograph.cs
```
using Raylib_cs;
using System.Numerics;
using VoxelEngine.MathTools;
using VoxelEngine.Scenes;
using VoxelEngine.World;

namespace Games.InstantCamera;

/// <summary>
/// A photograph in hand: the pieces of the world that were inside the frame, kept where they
/// stood, the pose they were seen from, and a picture of exactly those pieces. Putting it down
/// moves every piece by the rigid motion from that pose to the new one. Each piece keeps its own
/// grid and turns as a whole, so nothing is ever resampled into the world's grid, whatever the
/// angle.
///
/// The picture is rendered from the pieces themselves against an empty sky, not read off the
/// screen: what is on the print is what will be put down, and nothing beyond the photograph's
/// depth. It develops like instant film while the pieces are meshed in the background, which is
/// also why putting it down costs no meshing.
/// </summary>
internal sealed class Photograph : IDisposable
{
    /// <summary>Metres from the eye at which the photograph starts, so the frame never cuts through the head</summary>
    public const float Near = 0.3f;

    private const int PictureSize = 1024;

    /// <summary>Seconds the print takes to develop once its picture is rendered</summary>
    private const float DevelopSeconds = 1.2f;

    private readonly VoxelTerrainScene _scene;
    private readonly List<VoxelBody> _pieces;
    private readonly RenderTexture2D _picture;
    private bool _rendered;
    private bool _placed;
    private float _developed;

    /// <summary>Where the photograph was taken from</summary>
    public CameraPose Pose { get; }

    /// <summary>Tangent of half the opening of the square frame</summary>
    public float TanHalf { get; }

    /// <summary>How far into the scene the photograph reaches, in metres</summary>
    public float Depth { get; }

    private Photograph(VoxelTerrainScene scene, List<VoxelBody> pieces, CameraPose pose, float tanHalf, float depth)
    {
        _scene = scene;
        _pieces = pieces;
        Pose = pose;
        TanHalf = tanHalf;
        Depth = depth;

        _picture = Raylib.LoadRenderTexture(PictureSize, PictureSize);
        Raylib.SetTextureFilter(_picture.Texture, TextureFilter.Bilinear);
    }

    /// <summary>Photographs what the camera sees through a square frame, up to <paramref name="depth"/> metres</summary>
    public static Photograph Take(VoxelTerrainScene scene, Camera3D camera, float tanHalf, float depth)
    {
        CameraPose pose = CameraPose.FromCamera(camera);
        FrustumVolume volume = FrustumVolume.FromPose(pose, tanHalf, tanHalf, Near, depth);

        var pieces = new List<VoxelBody>();
        if (scene.World.CopyInside(volume, "photo") is { } terrain) pieces.Add(terrain);

        // Pieces of earlier photographs are copied in their own grids, so a photograph of a
        // photograph is as sharp as the first
        foreach (VoxelBody body in scene.Bodies)
            if (body.CopyInside(volume) is { } copy)
                pieces.Add(copy);

        return new Photograph(scene, pieces, pose, tanHalf, depth);
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// A frame's worth of developing: mesh the pieces within the frame's budget, render the
    /// picture once they are all meshed, then let it come through. Call it from Update.
    /// </summary>
    public void Develop(float dt)
    {
        if (!_rendered)
        {
            if (!_scene.PrepareBodies(_pieces)) return;

            _scene.RenderBodies(_picture, PictureCamera(), _pieces);
            _rendered = true;
        }

        _developed = MathF.Min(1f, _developed + dt / DevelopSeconds);
    }

    /// <summary>The camera the photograph was taken with: square, and exactly as wide as the frame</summary>
    private Camera3D PictureCamera() => new()
    {
        Position = Pose.Position,
        Target = Pose.Position + Pose.Forward,
        Up = Pose.Up,
        FovY = 2f * MathF.Atan(TanHalf) * 180f / MathF.PI,
        Projection = CameraProjection.Perspective,
    };

    /// <summary>
    /// Puts the photograph down as seen from <paramref name="target"/>: whatever stood inside the
    /// frame there is cut away, and the pieces take its place exactly as they were seen.
    /// </summary>
    public void Place(CameraPose target)
    {
        FrustumVolume volume = FrustumVolume.FromPose(target, TanHalf, TanHalf, Near, Depth);

        _scene.World.CarveInside(volume);
        foreach (VoxelBody body in _scene.Bodies.ToList())
            if (body.CarveInside(volume) > 0 && body.CountSolid() == 0)
                _scene.RemoveBody(body);

        // The meshes are in each piece's own frame, so moving a piece leaves them valid
        (Quaternion rotation, Vector3 translation) = CameraPose.Between(Pose, target);
        foreach (VoxelBody piece in _pieces)
        {
            piece.Position = Vector3.Transform(piece.Position, rotation) + translation;
            piece.Orientation = Quaternion.Normalize(rotation * piece.Orientation);
            _scene.AddBody(piece);
        }

        _placed = true;
    }

    /// <summary>The picture, rendered from the pieces; a render texture, so stored bottom row first</summary>
    public Texture2D Picture => _picture.Texture;

    /// <summary>How much of the undeveloped film still covers the picture, 1 to 0: it clears slowly, then all at once</summary>
    public float Cover => _rendered ? 1f - _developed * _developed * (3f - 2f * _developed) : 1f;

    /// <summary>Releases the picture, and the pieces' meshes if the photograph was never put down</summary>
    public void Dispose()
    {
        if (!_placed)
            foreach (VoxelBody piece in _pieces)
                _scene.RemoveBody(piece);

        Raylib.UnloadRenderTexture(_picture);
    }
}
```

### Games/InstantCamera/Scripts/ViewModelPainter.cs
```
using Raylib_cs;
using System.Numerics;
using VoxelEngine.MathTools;

namespace Games.InstantCamera;

/// <summary>Where an object sits in view space (x right, y up, -z ahead of the eye) and how it is turned</summary>
internal readonly record struct Placement(Vector3 Position, Quaternion Rotation)
{
    public static readonly Placement Identity = new(Vector3.Zero, Quaternion.Identity);

    /// <summary>A point in the object's own frame, in view space</summary>
    public Vector3 Apply(Vector3 local) => Position + Vector3.Transform(local, Rotation);

    /// <summary>Part of the way from <paramref name="from"/> to <paramref name="to"/></summary>
    public static Placement Lerp(Placement from, Placement to, float t)
        => new(Vector3.Lerp(from.Position, to.Position, t), Quaternion.Slerp(from.Rotation, to.Rotation, t));

    public Placement Moved(Vector3 offset) => this with { Position = Position + offset };
}

/// <summary>
/// Draws what the player holds in first person: shaded boxes and flat quads placed in view
/// space. Everything goes through a shader that squeezes its depth into the front thousandth of
/// the depth range, so what the player holds is always in front of the world, even with their
/// nose against a wall, while its parts still hide each other correctly.
/// </summary>
internal sealed class ViewModelPainter : IDisposable
{
    private const string FragmentSource = @"#version 330
in vec2 fragTexCoord;
in vec4 fragColor;
uniform sampler2D texture0;
uniform vec4 colDiffuse;
out vec4 finalColor;

void main()
{
    finalColor = texture(texture0, fragTexCoord) * colDiffuse * fragColor;
    gl_FragDepth = gl_FragCoord.z * 0.001;
}";

    /// <summary>Light for the held things, fixed to the view: from above, a little left and behind the player</summary>
    private static readonly Vector3 Light = Vector3.Normalize(new Vector3(-0.35f, 0.85f, 0.4f));

    // Faces of a unit box as normal and four corners, counter-clockwise seen from outside
    private static readonly (Vector3 Normal, Vector3[] Corners)[] Faces =
    {
        (Vector3.UnitY, new Vector3[] { new(0, 1, 1), new(1, 1, 1), new(1, 1, 0), new(0, 1, 0) }),
        (-Vector3.UnitY, new Vector3[] { new(0, 0, 0), new(1, 0, 0), new(1, 0, 1), new(0, 0, 1) }),
        (Vector3.UnitX, new Vector3[] { new(1, 0, 0), new(1, 1, 0), new(1, 1, 1), new(1, 0, 1) }),
        (-Vector3.UnitX, new Vector3[] { new(0, 0, 1), new(0, 1, 1), new(0, 1, 0), new(0, 0, 0) }),
        (Vector3.UnitZ, new Vector3[] { new(1, 0, 1), new(1, 1, 1), new(0, 1, 1), new(0, 0, 1) }),
        (-Vector3.UnitZ, new Vector3[] { new(0, 0, 0), new(0, 1, 0), new(1, 1, 0), new(1, 0, 0) }),
    };

    private readonly Shader _shader = Raylib.LoadShaderFromMemory(null, FragmentSource);
    private CameraPose _eye;

    /// <summary>Starts drawing held things for the eye at <paramref name="eye"/>; call inside BeginMode3D, after the world</summary>
    public void Begin(CameraPose eye)
    {
        _eye = eye;
        Rlgl.DrawRenderBatchActive();
        Raylib.BeginShaderMode(_shader);
        Rlgl.DisableBackfaceCulling(); // the depth test sorts the faces; no winding to get wrong
    }

    /// <summary>Everything drawn from here on lies over the rest, in order: overlays such as the viewfinder mask</summary>
    public void BeginOverlay()
    {
        Rlgl.DrawRenderBatchActive();
        Rlgl.DisableDepthTest();
    }

    public void End()
    {
        Rlgl.DrawRenderBatchActive();
        Rlgl.EnableDepthTest();
        Rlgl.EnableBackfaceCulling();
        Raylib.EndShaderMode();
    }

    /// <summary>A box from <paramref name="min"/> to <paramref name="max"/> in the frame of <paramref name="placement"/>, each face shaded by the view light</summary>
    public void Box(in Placement placement, Vector3 min, Vector3 max, Color color)
    {
        Vector3 size = max - min;

        Rlgl.SetTexture(0);
        Rlgl.Begin(DrawMode.Quads);

        foreach ((Vector3 normal, Vector3[] corners) in Faces)
        {
            float facing = Vector3.Dot(Vector3.Transform(normal, placement.Rotation), Light);
            float shade = 0.5f + 0.5f * MathF.Max(0f, facing);
            Rlgl.Color4ub((byte)(color.R * shade), (byte)(color.G * shade), (byte)(color.B * shade), color.A);

            foreach (Vector3 corner in corners)
                Vertex(placement.Apply(min + corner * size));
        }

        Rlgl.End();
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// A flat rectangle facing +z in the frame of <paramref name="placement"/>, lying at depth
    /// <paramref name="z"/>. With a texture it shows a render texture, stored bottom row first.
    /// </summary>
    public void Quad(in Placement placement, float left, float right, float bottom, float top, float z, Color tint, uint texture = 0)
    {
        if (tint.A == 0) return;

        Rlgl.SetTexture(texture);
        Rlgl.Begin(DrawMode.Quads);
        Rlgl.Color4ub(tint.R, tint.G, tint.B, tint.A);

        Rlgl.TexCoord2f(0f, 0f);
        Vertex(placement.Apply(new Vector3(left, bottom, z)));
        Rlgl.TexCoord2f(1f, 0f);
        Vertex(placement.Apply(new Vector3(right, bottom, z)));
        Rlgl.TexCoord2f(1f, 1f);
        Vertex(placement.Apply(new Vector3(right, top, z)));
        Rlgl.TexCoord2f(0f, 1f);
        Vertex(placement.Apply(new Vector3(left, top, z)));

        Rlgl.End();
        Rlgl.SetTexture(0);
    }

    private void Vertex(Vector3 view)
    {
        Vector3 world = _eye.ToWorld(view);
        Rlgl.Vertex3f(world.X, world.Y, world.Z);
    }

    public void Dispose() => Raylib.UnloadShader(_shader);
}
```

### Games/RocketStorm/Scripts/Highscores.cs
```
using VoxelEngine.Config;

namespace Games.RocketStorm;

/// <summary>One run that ended</summary>
public sealed class HighscoreEntry
{
    public int Score { get; set; }
    public int Wave { get; set; }
    public float Seconds { get; set; }
    public string Date { get; set; } = "";
}

/// <summary>
/// The best runs on this machine, kept in the settings store next to the game's dials. Ten
/// entries, best first; a new run is slotted in where it belongs.
/// </summary>
public sealed class Highscores
{
    private const string Key = "RocketStorm/Highscores";
    private const int Capacity = 10;

    private readonly SettingsStore _store;

    public List<HighscoreEntry> Entries { get; }

    public Highscores(SettingsStore store)
    {
        _store = store;
        Entries = store.Load<List<HighscoreEntry>>(Key, () => new List<HighscoreEntry>());
        Entries.Sort((a, b) => b.Score.CompareTo(a.Score));
    }

    public int Best => Entries.Count > 0 ? Entries[0].Score : 0;

    /// <summary>Records a run; returns its rank (1 = best) or 0 when it did not make the list</summary>
    public int Record(int score, int wave, float seconds)
    {
        var entry = new HighscoreEntry { Score = score, Wave = wave, Seconds = seconds, Date = DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm") };

        int rank = Entries.FindIndex(other => score > other.Score);
        if (rank < 0) rank = Entries.Count;
        if (rank >= Capacity) return 0;

        Entries.Insert(rank, entry);
        if (Entries.Count > Capacity) Entries.RemoveAt(Entries.Count - 1);

        _store.Save(Key, Entries);
        return rank + 1;
    }
}
```

### Games/RocketStorm/Scripts/Rocket.cs
```
using Raylib_cs;
using System.Numerics;
using VoxelEngine.Effects;

namespace Games.RocketStorm;

/// <summary>
/// A projectile on a scripted path (quadratic Bézier): launch, apex and impact are fixed the
/// moment it fires, which makes the point of impact predictable. The body is built from cubes,
/// tips into the direction of travel and trails smoke behind it.
/// </summary>
public sealed class Rocket
{
    private readonly Vector3 _start;
    private readonly Vector3 _control;
    private readonly Vector3 _target;
    private readonly float _flightSeconds;

    private float _elapsed;

    public Vector3 Position { get; private set; }
    public Vector3 Direction { get; private set; } = Vector3.UnitY;

    /// <summary>Impact reached; the caller then triggers the explosion</summary>
    public bool Landed => _elapsed >= _flightSeconds;

    /// <summary>Colour of the nose and fins; tells rocket types apart on screen</summary>
    public Color Accent { get; init; } = new(214, 78, 62, 255);

    /// <summary>Length of the body in metres; everything else scales with it</summary>
    public float Scale { get; init; } = 1f;

    public Rocket(Vector3 start, Vector3 apex, Vector3 target, float flightSeconds)
    {
        _start = start;
        _target = target;
        _flightSeconds = flightSeconds;

        // Control point chosen so the curve actually passes through the requested apex
        _control = 2f * apex - 0.5f * start - 0.5f * target;

        Position = start;
    }

    public void Update(float dt, ParticleSystem particles)
    {
        if (Landed) return;

        Vector3 previous = Position;

        _elapsed = MathF.Min(_elapsed + dt, _flightSeconds);
        float t = _elapsed / _flightSeconds;

        float inverse = 1f - t;
        Position = inverse * inverse * _start + 2f * inverse * t * _control + t * t * _target;

        Vector3 delta = Position - previous;
        if (delta.LengthSquared() > 1e-6f) Direction = Vector3.Normalize(delta);

        // Smoke sits at the tail and is pushed back against the direction of travel
        Vector3 nozzle = Position - Direction * (0.9f * Scale);
        particles.SpawnSmoke(nozzle, -Direction * 3.5f, 3, Scale);
    }

    public void Draw()
    {
        if (Landed) return;

        // Rotate into the direction of travel; the body's own axis is +Y
        Vector3 axis = Vector3.Cross(Vector3.UnitY, Direction);
        float angle = MathF.Acos(Math.Clamp(Vector3.Dot(Vector3.UnitY, Direction), -1f, 1f)) * (180f / MathF.PI);
        if (axis.LengthSquared() < 1e-6f) axis = Vector3.UnitX;

        Rlgl.PushMatrix();
        Rlgl.Translatef(Position.X, Position.Y, Position.Z);
        Rlgl.Rotatef(angle, axis.X, axis.Y, axis.Z);
        Rlgl.Scalef(Scale, Scale, Scale);

        Raylib.DrawCubeV(new Vector3(0f, 0f, 0f), new Vector3(0.6f, 1.6f, 0.6f), new Color(228, 232, 240, 255));
        Raylib.DrawCubeV(new Vector3(0f, 1.05f, 0f), new Vector3(0.42f, 0.5f, 0.42f), Accent);
        Raylib.DrawCubeV(new Vector3(0f, -0.95f, 0f), new Vector3(0.34f, 0.3f, 0.34f), new Color(90, 96, 110, 255));

        // Fins
        Raylib.DrawCubeV(new Vector3(0.42f, -0.6f, 0f), new Vector3(0.3f, 0.5f, 0.12f), Accent);
        Raylib.DrawCubeV(new Vector3(-0.42f, -0.6f, 0f), new Vector3(0.3f, 0.5f, 0.12f), Accent);
        Raylib.DrawCubeV(new Vector3(0f, -0.6f, 0.42f), new Vector3(0.12f, 0.5f, 0.3f), Accent);
        Raylib.DrawCubeV(new Vector3(0f, -0.6f, -0.42f), new Vector3(0.12f, 0.5f, 0.3f), Accent);

        Rlgl.PopMatrix();
    }
}
```

### Games/RocketStorm/Scripts/RocketBarrage.cs
```
using Raylib_cs;
using System.Numerics;
using VoxelEngine.Audio;
using VoxelEngine.Scenes;

namespace Games.RocketStorm;

/// <summary>What is coming in: each kind asks something different of the player</summary>
public enum RocketKind
{
    /// <summary>Arcs in from the horizon; a hole in the ground is cover</summary>
    Standard,

    /// <summary>Splits over the target into three small ones; cover has to be wide</summary>
    Cluster,

    /// <summary>Slow and heavy, twice the crater and twice the reach; get far away or shoot it</summary>
    Buster,

    /// <summary>Flies low and follows you; only the flak stops it</summary>
    Seeker,
}

/// <summary>
/// The attack: waves of rockets that arc in on the player from the horizon, each announced by a
/// ring on the ground that contracts until it arrives, plus seekers that fly low and turn after
/// the player. Every wave sends more and worse of them. The flak takes anything down mid-air.
/// </summary>
public sealed class RocketBarrage
{
    private const float FlightSeconds = 2.6f;
    private const float ApexHeight = 34f;
    private const float LaunchDistance = 120f;

    private const float SeekerSpeed = 11f;
    private const float SeekerTurnPerSecond = 1.4f;
    private const float SeekerAltitude = 2.2f;
    private const float SeekerFuse = 2.6f;

    private readonly RocketStormSettings _settings;

    private sealed class Incoming
    {
        public required Rocket Rocket { get; init; }
        public required RocketKind Kind { get; init; }
        public required Vector3 Impact { get; init; }
        public required float Flight { get; init; }
        public float TimeLeft;
        public bool Split;
    }

    private sealed class Seeker
    {
        public Vector3 Position;
        public Vector3 Heading;
        public float Life = 40f;
    }

    /// <summary>A rocket or seeker was destroyed in the air: where, and what kind</summary>
    public event Action<Vector3, RocketKind>? ShotDown;

    /// <summary>Something reached the player or the ground: where, with what crater radius and damage scale</summary>
    public event Action<Vector3, float, float>? Impact;

    private readonly VoxelTerrainScene _scene;
    private readonly AudioBank _audio;
    private readonly List<Incoming> _live = new();
    private readonly List<Seeker> _seekers = new();

    private int _remainingInWave;
    private float _nextLaunch;
    private float _waveBreak = 2.5f;

    public int Wave { get; private set; }

    public int InFlight => _live.Count + _seekers.Count;

    /// <summary>Seconds until the next wave; 0 while one is running</summary>
    public float WaveBreak => _waveBreak;

    /// <summary>Crater radius of a standard rocket, and with it the zone where a hit lands at full force</summary>
    public float CraterRadius => _settings.CraterRadius;

    public RocketBarrage(VoxelTerrainScene scene, AudioBank audio, RocketStormSettings settings)
    {
        _scene = scene;
        _audio = audio;
        _settings = settings;
    }

    public void Reset()
    {
        _live.Clear();
        _seekers.Clear();
        Wave = 0;
        _remainingInWave = 0;
        _waveBreak = 2.5f;
    }

    public void Update(float dt, Vector3 target)
    {
        UpdateSchedule(dt, target);
        UpdateRockets(dt);
        UpdateSeekers(dt, target);
    }

    private void UpdateRockets(float dt)
    {
        for (int i = _live.Count - 1; i >= 0; i--)
        {
            Incoming incoming = _live[i];
            incoming.TimeLeft = MathF.Max(0f, incoming.TimeLeft - dt);
            incoming.Rocket.Update(dt, _scene.Particles);

            // A cluster rocket breaks up over the target: three small ones rain down around it
            if (incoming.Kind == RocketKind.Cluster && !incoming.Split && incoming.TimeLeft < incoming.Flight * 0.45f)
            {
                incoming.Split = true;
                _live.RemoveAt(i);
                Scatter(incoming);
                continue;
            }

            if (!incoming.Rocket.Landed) continue;

            _live.RemoveAt(i);
            Impact?.Invoke(incoming.Impact, CraterOf(incoming.Kind), DamageOf(incoming.Kind));
        }
    }

    private void Scatter(Incoming parent)
    {
        for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
        {
            float angle = i / 3f * MathF.Tau + Random.Shared.NextSingle();
            Vector3 impact = OnGround(parent.Impact + new Vector3(MathF.Cos(angle) * 5f, 0f, MathF.Sin(angle) * 5f));
            Vector3 start = parent.Rocket.Position;
            var apex = new Vector3((start.X + impact.X) * 0.5f, MathF.Max(start.Y, impact.Y) + 6f, (start.Z + impact.Z) * 0.5f);

            _live.Add(new Incoming
            {
                Rocket = new Rocket(start, apex, impact, 1.1f) { Scale = 0.55f, Accent = new Color(255, 170, 60, 255) },
                Kind = RocketKind.Standard,
                Impact = impact,
                Flight = 1.1f,
                TimeLeft = 1.1f,
            });
        }

        _audio.Play("launch", 0.3f, 1.4f);
    }

    private void UpdateSeekers(float dt, Vector3 target)
    {
        for (int i = _seekers.Count - 1; i >= 0; i--)
        {
            Seeker seeker = _seekers[i];
            seeker.Life -= dt;

            // Turn towards the player at a limited rate, so it can be outrun for a moment
            Vector3 toTarget = target + Vector3.UnitY - seeker.Position;
            float distance = toTarget.Length();
            Vector3 wanted = distance > 1e-3f ? toTarget / distance : seeker.Heading;
            seeker.Heading = Vector3.Normalize(Vector3.Lerp(seeker.Heading, wanted, MathF.Min(1f, SeekerTurnPerSecond * dt)));

            seeker.Position += seeker.Heading * SeekerSpeed * dt;

            // Hug the ground: never below the surface, never far above it
            float floor = _scene.SurfaceHeight(seeker.Position.X, seeker.Position.Z) + SeekerAltitude;
            seeker.Position.Y = MathF.Max(seeker.Position.Y, floor);
            seeker.Position.Y += (floor - seeker.Position.Y) * MathF.Min(1f, 2f * dt);

            _scene.Particles.SpawnTrail(seeker.Position, -seeker.Heading * 2f, new Color(255, 80, 60, 255), 0.25f);

            if (distance < SeekerFuse || seeker.Life <= 0f)
            {
                _seekers.RemoveAt(i);
                Impact?.Invoke(seeker.Position, CraterOf(RocketKind.Seeker), DamageOf(RocketKind.Seeker));
            }
        }
    }

    private void UpdateSchedule(float dt, Vector3 target)
    {
        if (_remainingInWave <= 0)
        {
            // The gap between waves is the time you get to dig in
            if (InFlight > 0) return;

            _waveBreak -= dt;
            if (_waveBreak > 0f) return;

            Wave++;
            _remainingInWave = _settings.RocketsInFirstWave + Wave - 1;
            _nextLaunch = 0f;
            _waveBreak = 0f;
            return;
        }

        _nextLaunch -= dt;
        if (_nextLaunch > 0f) return;

        Launch(target, PickKind());
        _remainingInWave--;

        _nextLaunch = MathF.Max(0.35f, 1.5f - Wave * 0.08f);
        if (_remainingInWave == 0) _waveBreak = _settings.WavePause;
    }

    /// <summary>The mix hardens with the waves: clusters from the second, seekers from the third, busters from the fourth</summary>
    private RocketKind PickKind()
    {
        float roll = Random.Shared.NextSingle();

        if (Wave >= 4 && roll < 0.15f) return RocketKind.Buster;
        if (Wave >= 3 && roll < 0.40f) return RocketKind.Seeker;
        if (Wave >= 2 && roll < 0.65f) return RocketKind.Cluster;

        return RocketKind.Standard;
    }

    private void Launch(Vector3 target, RocketKind kind)
    {
        // Spread the impacts: some land right on the player, others are warning shots
        float angle = Random.Shared.NextSingle() * MathF.Tau;
        float spread = 2f + Random.Shared.NextSingle() * (6f + Wave);
        var offset = new Vector3(MathF.Cos(angle) * spread, 0f, MathF.Sin(angle) * spread);

        Vector3 impact = OnGround(target + offset);

        float fromAngle = Random.Shared.NextSingle() * MathF.Tau;
        Vector3 pad = OnGround(impact + new Vector3(
            MathF.Cos(fromAngle) * LaunchDistance, 0f, MathF.Sin(fromAngle) * LaunchDistance));

        if (kind == RocketKind.Seeker)
        {
            _seekers.Add(new Seeker
            {
                Position = pad + Vector3.UnitY * SeekerAltitude,
                Heading = Vector3.Normalize(new Vector3(impact.X - pad.X, 0f, impact.Z - pad.Z)),
            });
            _audio.Play("launch", 0.3f, 1.6f);
            return;
        }

        float flight = kind == RocketKind.Buster ? FlightSeconds * 1.7f : FlightSeconds;
        var apex = new Vector3(
            (pad.X + impact.X) * 0.5f,
            MathF.Max(pad.Y, impact.Y) + ApexHeight * (kind == RocketKind.Buster ? 1.5f : 1f),
            (pad.Z + impact.Z) * 0.5f);

        var rocket = new Rocket(pad + Vector3.UnitY * 1.2f, apex, impact, flight)
        {
            Scale = kind switch { RocketKind.Buster => 2.2f, RocketKind.Cluster => 1.3f, _ => 1f },
            Accent = kind switch
            {
                RocketKind.Buster => new Color(90, 90, 100, 255),
                RocketKind.Cluster => new Color(255, 170, 60, 255),
                _ => new Color(214, 78, 62, 255),
            },
        };

        _live.Add(new Incoming { Rocket = rocket, Kind = kind, Impact = impact, Flight = flight, TimeLeft = flight });

        _audio.Play("launch", 0.35f, kind == RocketKind.Buster ? 0.6f : 0.9f + Random.Shared.NextSingle() * 0.2f);
    }

    private float CraterOf(RocketKind kind) => kind switch
    {
        RocketKind.Buster => _settings.CraterRadius * 2f,
        RocketKind.Seeker => _settings.CraterRadius * 0.7f,
        _ => _settings.CraterRadius,
    };

    private static float DamageOf(RocketKind kind) => kind switch
    {
        RocketKind.Buster => 2f,
        RocketKind.Seeker => 0.8f,
        _ => 1f,
    };

    private Vector3 OnGround(Vector3 position)
        => new(position.X, _scene.SurfaceHeight(position.X, position.Z), position.Z);

    /// <summary>
    /// The flak: whatever flies closest to the aim line within its reach goes up. Returns where
    /// it went up, or null for a miss.
    /// </summary>
    public Vector3? ShootDown(Ray aim, float reach)
    {
        Vector3 direction = Vector3.Normalize(aim.Direction);
        float best = float.MaxValue;
        int bestRocket = -1, bestSeeker = -1;

        for (int i = 0; i < _live.Count; i++)
        {
            float miss = MissDistance(aim.Position, direction, _live[i].Rocket.Position, reach);
            float slack = 2f + _live[i].Rocket.Scale;
            if (miss < slack && miss < best) { best = miss; bestRocket = i; bestSeeker = -1; }
        }

        for (int i = 0; i < _seekers.Count; i++)
        {
            float miss = MissDistance(aim.Position, direction, _seekers[i].Position, reach);
            if (miss < 2f && miss < best) { best = miss; bestSeeker = i; bestRocket = -1; }
        }

        if (bestRocket >= 0)
        {
            Incoming hit = _live[bestRocket];
            _live.RemoveAt(bestRocket);
            ShotDown?.Invoke(hit.Rocket.Position, hit.Kind);
            return hit.Rocket.Position;
        }

        if (bestSeeker >= 0)
        {
            Seeker hit = _seekers[bestSeeker];
            _seekers.RemoveAt(bestSeeker);
            ShotDown?.Invoke(hit.Position, RocketKind.Seeker);
            return hit.Position;
        }

        return null;
    }

    /// <summary>Distance of a point from the aim line, or infinity when it lies behind the muzzle or past the reach</summary>
    private static float MissDistance(Vector3 origin, Vector3 direction, Vector3 point, float reach)
    {
        Vector3 relative = point - origin;
        float along = Vector3.Dot(relative, direction);
        if (along < 0f || along > reach) return float.MaxValue;

        return (relative - direction * along).Length();
    }

    public void Draw3D()
    {
        foreach (Incoming incoming in _live)
        {
            incoming.Rocket.Draw();
            DrawMarker(incoming);
        }

        foreach (Seeker seeker in _seekers)
        {
            Raylib.DrawCubeV(seeker.Position, new Vector3(0.9f, 0.5f, 0.9f), new Color(40, 40, 50, 255));
            Raylib.DrawCubeV(seeker.Position + seeker.Heading * 0.5f, new Vector3(0.35f), new Color(255, 70, 50, 255));
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Target ring: contracts onto the impact point and blinks faster towards the end</summary>
    private void DrawMarker(Incoming incoming)
    {
        float progress = 1f - incoming.TimeLeft / incoming.Flight;
        float crater = CraterOf(incoming.Kind);
        float radius = crater * (1.9f - progress);

        float blink = MathF.Sin((float)Raylib.GetTime() * (6f + progress * 18f));
        byte alpha = (byte)(120 + 100 * (blink * 0.5f + 0.5f));

        Vector3 center = incoming.Impact + Vector3.UnitY * 0.15f;
        var color = new Color((byte)255, (byte)(90 + 60 * (1f - progress)), (byte)70, alpha);

        Raylib.DrawCircle3D(center, radius, Vector3.UnitX, 90f, color);
        Raylib.DrawCircle3D(center, crater, Vector3.UnitX, 90f, new Color((byte)255, (byte)70, (byte)60, (byte)70));
    }
}
```

### Games/RocketStorm/Scripts/RocketStormGame.cs
```
using Raylib_cs;
using System.Numerics;
using VoxelEngine.Audio;
using VoxelEngine.Core;
using VoxelEngine.Scenes;
using VoxelEngine.UI;
using VoxelEngine.World;

namespace Games.RocketStorm;

/// <summary>
/// Survival under fire. The world runs in Smooth mode and every impact tears a round crater out
/// of the ground. The sphere brush stays live, so cover is something you dig for yourself:
/// sitting in a hole you can ride out a direct hit next to you. Later waves bring clusters,
/// seekers and busters, the flak on F takes them down for points, and the best runs are kept.
/// </summary>
[GameDefinition("RocketStorm", "Rocket Storm", "Survive the barrage - you dig your own cover")]
public sealed class RocketStormGame : Game
{
    private const float MaxHealth = 100f;
    private const string SettingsKey = "RocketStorm/Game";

    /// <summary>Fixed sun, slightly off the zenith so slopes and craters keep their contrast</summary>
    private const float SunAngle = 75f;

    private const float FlakReach = 160f;
    private const float FlakCooldown = 0.45f;
    private const int ScorePerSecond = 10;

    private VoxelTerrainScene _scene = null!;
    private RocketBarrage _barrage = null!;
    private RocketStormSettings _settings = null!;
    private Highscores _highscores = null!;
    private TuningSection? _tuning;

    private float _health = MaxHealth;
    private float _survived;
    private float _hurtFlash;
    private bool _dead;
    private float _score;
    private int _shotDown;
    private int _rank;
    private float _flakHeat;
    private float _tracerLife;
    private Vector3 _tracerFrom, _tracerTo;
    private float _hitMarker;
    private int _lastBonus;

    public override Camera3D Camera => _scene.Camera;

    public override IReadOnlyList<string> ControlHints => new[]
    {
        "WASD + mouse: run, Shift sprints, Space jumps",
        "LMB: dig, a hole in the ground is cover | RMB: place material",
        "F: flak - shoots down whatever you aim at, seekers only die this way",
        "Standard rockets arc in, clusters split, busters dig deep, seekers hunt you",
        "R: start over once they got you",
    };

    public override void Load()
    {
        // Flatter than the sandbox: the craters should shape the landscape instead of
        // disappearing into a mountainside
        var terrain = new DefaultTerrainGenerator
        {
            Seed = 7331,
            BaseHeight = 14,
            ContinentAmplitude = 10f,
            MountainAmplitude = 7f,
            DetailAmplitude = 3f,
        };

        _scene = new VoxelTerrainScene(Context, new VoxelTerrainOptions
        {
            SaveSlot = "rocket-storm",
            Spawn = new Vector3(128, 60, 128),
            Generator = terrain,

            // A flat arena needs no mountains, and a low world streams and meshes that much faster
            WorldHeight = 64,
            Mode = TerrainMode.Smooth,
            RunDayNight = false,
            SunAngleDegrees = SunAngle,
        });

        Context.Audio.Define("launch", SfxShape.Launch);
        Context.Audio.Define("explosion", SfxShape.Explosion);
        Context.Audio.Define("hit", SfxShape.Hit);
        Context.Audio.Define("flak", SfxShape.Shot);
        Context.Audio.Define("shotdown", new SfxShape(0.45f, 520f, 90f, 0.7f, 7f));

        _highscores = new Highscores(Context.Store);

        _settings = Context.Store.Load<RocketStormSettings>(SettingsKey);
        var defaults = new RocketStormSettings();
        _tuning = Context.Tuning.AddSection("ROCKET STORM", () => Context.Store.Save(SettingsKey, _settings))
            .Value("Wave pause s", () => _settings.WavePause, v => _settings.WavePause = v, defaults.WavePause, 0.5f, 1f, 20f, "0.0")
            .Value("Rockets in wave 1", () => _settings.RocketsInFirstWave, v => _settings.RocketsInFirstWave = (int)v, defaults.RocketsInFirstWave, 1f, 1f, 12f, "0")
            .Value("Crater radius", () => _settings.CraterRadius, v => _settings.CraterRadius = v, defaults.CraterRadius, 0.5f, 1.5f, 12f, "0.0")
            .Value("Damage radius", () => _settings.DamageRadius, v => _settings.DamageRadius = v, defaults.DamageRadius, 1f, 3f, 40f, "0");

        _barrage = new RocketBarrage(_scene, Context.Audio, _settings);
        _barrage.Impact += OnImpact;
        _barrage.ShotDown += OnShotDown;
    }

    public override void Update(float dt)
    {
        _scene.ShowDebugGeometry = Context.DebugOverlay;
        _hurtFlash = MathF.Max(0f, _hurtFlash - dt * 1.6f);

        if (_dead)
        {
            if (Raylib.IsKeyPressed(KeyboardKey.R)) Restart();

            // The world keeps running so the picture does not freeze, but the player is out:
            // the scene ignores movement and mouse look while AllowPlayerControl is off
            _scene.Update(dt);
            return;
        }

        _survived += dt;
        _score += ScorePerSecond * dt;
        _flakHeat = MathF.Max(0f, _flakHeat - dt);
        _tracerLife = MathF.Max(0f, _tracerLife - dt);
        _hitMarker = MathF.Max(0f, _hitMarker - dt);

        if (Raylib.IsKeyPressed(KeyboardKey.F) && _flakHeat <= 0f) FireFlak();

        _barrage.Update(dt, _scene.Player.Position);
        _scene.Update(dt);
    }

    /// <summary>The flak: a hit-scan along the crosshair, with a tracer so a miss still shows where it went</summary>
    private void FireFlak()
    {
        _flakHeat = FlakCooldown;

        Ray aim = Raylib.GetScreenToWorldRay(new Vector2(Context.ScreenWidth / 2, Context.ScreenHeight / 2), _scene.Camera);
        Vector3 direction = Vector3.Normalize(aim.Direction);
        Vector3? hit = _barrage.ShootDown(aim, FlakReach);

        _tracerFrom = aim.Position + direction * 0.6f - Vector3.UnitY * 0.25f;
        _tracerTo = hit ?? aim.Position + direction * FlakReach;
        _tracerLife = 0.18f;

        _scene.AddLight(_tracerFrom + direction * 1.5f, 10f, new Vector3(1.8f, 1.4f, 0.8f), 0.12f);
        _scene.Particles.SpawnTrail(_tracerFrom + direction * 1.2f, direction * 6f, new Color(255, 220, 150, 255), 0.35f, 6);
        _scene.Shake(0.12f);
        Context.Audio.Play("flak", 0.5f, 1.3f);
    }

    private void OnShotDown(Vector3 position, RocketKind kind)
    {
        int bonus = kind switch { RocketKind.Buster => 300, RocketKind.Seeker => 200, RocketKind.Cluster => 150, _ => 100 };
        _score += bonus;
        _shotDown++;
        _lastBonus = bonus;
        _hitMarker = 0.8f;

        _scene.Particles.SpawnExplosion(position, new Color(255, 200, 120, 255), 0.9f);
        _scene.AddLight(position, 14f, new Vector3(1.6f, 1.0f, 0.5f), 0.4f);
        Context.Audio.PlayAt("shotdown", Vector3.Distance(_scene.Player.Position, position), 200f, 0.8f);
    }

    private void OnImpact(Vector3 impact, float craterRadius, float damageScale)
    {
        _scene.Explode(impact, craterRadius);

        float distance = Vector3.Distance(_scene.Player.Position, impact);
        _scene.Shake(MathF.Max(0f, 1.6f - distance / 18f) * damageScale);
        Context.Audio.PlayAt("explosion", distance, 90f, 0.9f, damageScale > 1.5f ? 0.7f : 1f);

        float damageRadius = _settings.DamageRadius * MathF.Sqrt(damageScale);
        if (distance >= damageRadius) return;

        // Close in it hurts badly, further out the damage falls off fast
        float falloff = 1f - distance / damageRadius;
        Damage(70f * damageScale * falloff * falloff);
    }

    private void Damage(float amount)
    {
        if (amount <= 0.5f) return;

        _health -= amount;
        _hurtFlash = Math.Clamp(amount / 45f, 0.25f, 1f);
        Context.Audio.Play("hit", 0.6f);

        if (_health > 0f) return;

        _health = 0f;
        _dead = true;
        _scene.AllowPlayerControl = false;
        _scene.Particles.SpawnExplosion(_scene.Player.Position + Vector3.UnitY, new Color(200, 60, 60, 255), 1.1f);

        _rank = _highscores.Record((int)_score, Math.Max(1, _barrage.Wave), _survived);
    }

    private void Restart()
    {
        _health = MaxHealth;
        _survived = 0f;
        _score = 0f;
        _shotDown = 0;
        _rank = 0;
        _dead = false;
        _hurtFlash = 0f;
        _scene.AllowPlayerControl = true;
        _barrage.Reset();
    }

    public override void UpdateAlways() => _scene.PumpMeshUploads();

    public override void DrawBackground() => _scene.DrawBackground();

    public override void DrawWorld()
    {
        _scene.Draw();
        if (!_dead) _barrage.Draw3D();

        if (_tracerLife > 0f)
        {
            // A glowing rod rather than a hairline, so a miss still shows where the shot went
            byte alpha = (byte)(255 * _tracerLife / 0.18f);
            Raylib.DrawCylinderEx(_tracerFrom, _tracerTo, 0.09f, 0.04f, 6, new Color((byte)255, (byte)235, (byte)170, alpha));
            Raylib.BeginBlendMode(BlendMode.Additive);
            Raylib.DrawCylinderEx(_tracerFrom, _tracerTo, 0.25f, 0.12f, 6, new Color((byte)255, (byte)160, (byte)60, (byte)(alpha / 3)));
            Raylib.EndBlendMode();
        }
    }

    public override void DrawHud()
    {
        int width = Context.ScreenWidth;
        int height = Context.ScreenHeight;

        if (_hurtFlash > 0f)
            Raylib.DrawRectangle(0, 0, width, height, new Color((byte)200, (byte)30, (byte)30, (byte)(90 * _hurtFlash)));

        Color healthColor = _health > 50f
            ? new Color(120, 220, 140, 255)
            : _health > 20f ? new Color(240, 200, 90, 255) : new Color(230, 90, 80, 255);

        Hud.Bar(16, height - 46, 260, 26, _health / MaxHealth, healthColor, $"{_health:0} HP");

        Hud.Text($"Wave {Math.Max(1, _barrage.Wave)}", 16, 40, 24, Hud.Accent);
        Hud.Text($"Score {(int)_score}  (best {_highscores.Best})", 16, 70, 20);
        Hud.Text($"Survived {_survived:0.0}s | shot down {_shotDown}", 16, 96, 18);

        if (_barrage.InFlight > 0)
            Hud.Text($"Incoming: {_barrage.InFlight}", 16, 122, 20, Hud.Warning);
        else if (_barrage.WaveBreak > 0f && !_dead)
            Hud.Text($"Next wave in {_barrage.WaveBreak:0.0}s", 16, 122, 20, Hud.Accent);

        Hud.Crosshair(width, height, _flakHeat > 0f ? new Color(255, 150, 90, 255) : null);
        Hud.Bar(width / 2 - 40, height / 2 + 24, 80, 6, 1f - _flakHeat / FlakCooldown, new Color(255, 200, 120, 255));
        Hud.Centered("F: FLAK", width / 2, height / 2 + 34, 14, new Color(255, 200, 120, 220));

        if (_hitMarker > 0f)
        {
            float rise = (0.8f - _hitMarker) * 40f;
            Hud.Centered($"+{_lastBonus}  shot down", width / 2, height / 2 - 40 - (int)rise, 22,
                new Color((byte)255, (byte)230, (byte)150, (byte)(255 * MathF.Min(1f, _hitMarker / 0.3f))));
        }

        Hud.Centered("LMB dig for cover | RMB build | F: flak shoots down the rocket under the crosshair | ESC menu", width / 2, height - 40, 16);

        if (!_dead) return;

        const int panelWidth = 460;
        int panelHeight = 150 + Math.Min(5, _highscores.Entries.Count) * 22;
        int x = (width - panelWidth) / 2;
        int y = (height - panelHeight) / 2;

        Hud.Panel(x, y, panelWidth, panelHeight);
        Hud.Centered("YOU DIDN'T MAKE IT", width / 2, y + 22, 30, Hud.Warning);
        Hud.Centered($"{(int)_score} points | {_survived:0.0} seconds, wave {Math.Max(1, _barrage.Wave)}" + (_rank > 0 ? $" | rank {_rank}" : ""), width / 2, y + 62, 20);

        int line = y + 96;
        for (int i = 0; i < Math.Min(5, _highscores.Entries.Count); i++)
        {
            HighscoreEntry entry = _highscores.Entries[i];
            bool mine = _rank == i + 1;
            Hud.Text($"{i + 1}.  {entry.Score,6}   wave {entry.Wave,2}   {entry.Seconds,5:0}s   {entry.Date}", x + 24, line, 18, mine ? Hud.Accent : Hud.Ink);
            line += 22;
        }

        Hud.Centered("R: try again | ESC: menu", width / 2, y + panelHeight - 34, 18, Hud.Accent);
    }

    public override void Unload()
    {
        if (_tuning != null) Context.Tuning.RemoveSection(_tuning);
        _scene.Dispose();
    }
}
```
